Padded up to a multiple of TILE so the\n// vectorized kernel never needs a scalar remainder loop; padding entries are\n// never used as force sources and their results are discarded.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    size_t n = 0;    // number of real bodies\n    size_t nPad = 0; // n rounded up to a multiple of TILE\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz;\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz;\n\n    explicit BodySystem(const size_t numBodies)\n        : n(numBodies), nPad(((numBodies + TILE - 1) / TILE) * TILE), px(nPad, 0.0), py(nPad, 0.0), pz(nPad, 0.0),\n          vx(nPad, 0.0), vy(nPad, 0.0), vz(nPad, 0.0) {}\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: reproduces the exact rand_r() stream of the original.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    const size_t nPad = bodies.nPad;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < nPad; i0 += TILE) {\n        double xi[TILE], yi[TILE], zi[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            xi[t] = px[i0 + t];\n            yi[t] = py[i0 + t];\n            zi[t] = pz[i0 + t];\n            Fx[t] = 0.0;\n            Fy[t] = 0.0;\n            Fz[t] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j];\n            const double yj = py[j];\n            const double zj = pz[j];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[t];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[t];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[t];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[t] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[t] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[t] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t tEnd = (i0 + TILE <= n) ? TILE : n - i0;\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < tEnd; ++t) {\n            vx[i0 + t] += DT * Fx[t];\n            vy[i0 + t] += DT * Fy[t];\n            vz[i0 + t] += DT * Fz[t];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n#pragma omp parallel for simd reduction(+ : energy) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (vx[i] * vx[i] + vy[i] * vy[i] + vz[i] * vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies).\n    // The i-loop is triangular, so a dynamic schedule balances the work.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : energy) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double local = 0.0;\n#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : local)\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - px[i];\n            const double dy = py[j] - py[i];\n            const double dz = pz[j] - pz[i];\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            local -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += local;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    int failure = 0; // 1: non-finite, 2: position bounds, 3: velocity bounds\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    // Report the first (lowest-index) failure, as the sequential version did.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : failure) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        int f = 0;\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px[i]) || !std::isfinite(py[i]) || !std::isfinite(pz[i]) || !std::isfinite(vx[i]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy[i]) || !std::isfinite(vz[i])) {\n            f = 1;\n        } else {\n            // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n            const double maxPos = 1e6;\n            const double maxVel = 1e6;\n            if (std::abs(px[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(py[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(pz[i]) > maxPos) {\n                f = 2;\n            } else if (std::abs(vx[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(vy[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(vz[i]) > maxVel) {\n                f = 3;\n            }\n        }\n        if (f != 0 && (failure == 0 || f < failure)) {\n            failure = f;\n        }\n    }\n\n    switch (failure) {\n        case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n        case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        case 3: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        default: return true;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; Padded up to a multiple of TILE so the\n// vectorized kernel never needs a scalar remainder loop; padding entries are\n// never used as force sources and their results are discarded.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    size_t n = 0;    // number of real bodies\n    size_t nPad = 0; // n rounded up to a multiple of TILE\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz;\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz;\n\n    explicit BodySystem(const size_t numBodies)\n        : n(numBodies), nPad(((numBodies + TILE - 1) / TILE) * TILE), px(nPad, 0.0), py(nPad, 0.0), pz(nPad, 0.0),\n          vx(nPad, 0.0), vy(nPad, 0.0), vz(nPad, 0.0) {}\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: reproduces the exact rand_r() stream of the original.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    const size_t nPad = bodies.nPad;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < nPad; i0 += TILE) {\n        double xi[TILE], yi[TILE], zi[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            xi[t] = px[i0 + t];\n            yi[t] = py[i0 + t];\n            zi[t] = pz[i0 + t];\n            Fx[t] = 0.0;\n            Fy[t] = 0.0;\n            Fz[t] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j];\n            const double yj = py[j];\n            const double zj = pz[j];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[t];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[t];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[t];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[t] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[t] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[t] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t tEnd = (i0 + TILE <= n) ? TILE : n - i0;\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < tEnd; ++t) {\n            vx[i0 + t] += DT * Fx[t];\n            vy[i0 + t] += DT * Fy[t];\n            vz[i0 + t] += DT * Fz[t];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n#pragma omp parallel for simd reduction(+ : energy) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (vx[i] * vx[i] + vy[i] * vy[i] + vz[i] * vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies).\n    // The i-loop is triangular, so a dynamic schedule balances the work.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : energy) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double local = 0.0;\n#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : local)\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - px[i];\n            const double dy = py[j] - py[i];\n            const double dz = pz[j] - pz[i];\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            local -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += local;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    int failure = 0; // 1: non-finite, 2: position bounds, 3: velocity bounds\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    // Report the first (lowest-index) failure, as the sequential version did.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : failure) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; return false;\n        default: return true;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// Number of bodies processed simultaneously by the vectorized inner kernel.\n// Each lane accumulates the forces of one body over all j in the original\n// order, so results are bit-identical to the sequential code.\nconstexpr size_t TILE = 16;\n\n// Structure-of-arrays body storage. Padded up to a multiple of TILE so the\n// vectorized kernel never needs a scalar remainder loop; padding entries are\n// never used as force sources and their results are discarded.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    size_t n = 0;    // number of real bodies\n    size_t nPad = 0; // n rounded up to a multiple of TILE\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz;\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz;\n\n    explicit BodySystem(const size_t numBodies)\n        : n(numBodies), nPad(((numBodies + TILE - 1) / TILE) * TILE), px(nPad, 0.0), py(nPad, 0.0), pz(nPad, 0.0),\n          vx(nPad, 0.0), vy(nPad, 0.0), vz(nPad, 0.0) {}\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: reproduces the exact rand_r() stream of the original.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    const size_t nPad = bodies.nPad;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < nPad; i0 += TILE) {\n        double xi[TILE], yi[TILE], zi[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            xi[t] = px[i0 + t];\n            yi[t] = py[i0 + t];\n            zi[t] = pz[i0 + t];\n            Fx[t] = 0.0;\n            Fy[t] = 0.0;\n            Fz[t] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j];\n            const double yj = py[j];\n            const double zj = pz[j];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[t];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[t];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[t];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[t] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[t] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[t] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t tEnd = (i0 + TILE <= n) ? TILE : n - i0;\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < tEnd; ++t) {\n            vx[i0 + t] += DT * Fx[t];\n            vy[i0 + t] += DT * Fy[t];\n            vz[i0 + t] += DT * Fz[t];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n#pragma omp parallel for simd reduction(+ : energy) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (vx[i] * vx[i] + vy[i] * vy[i] + vz[i] * vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies).\n    // The i-loop is triangular, so a dynamic schedule balances the work.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : energy) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double local = 0.0;\n#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : local)\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - px[i];\n            const double dy = py[j] - py[i];\n            const double dz = pz[j] - pz[i];\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            local -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += local;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    int failure = 0; // 1: non-finite, 2: position bounds, 3: velocity bounds\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    // Report the first (lowest-index) failure, as the sequential version did.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : failure) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        int f = 0;\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px[i]) || !std::isfinite(py[i]) || !std::isfinite(pz[i]) || !std::isfinite(vx[i]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy[i]) || !std::isfinite(vz[i])) {\n            f = 1;\n        } else {\n            // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n            const double maxPos = 1e6;\n            const double maxVel = 1e6;\n            if (std::abs(px[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(py[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(pz[i]) > maxPos) {\n                f = 2;\n            } else if (std::abs(vx[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(vy[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(vz[i]) > maxVel) {\n                f = 3;\n            }\n        }\n        if (f != 0 && (failure == 0 || f < failure)) {\n            failure = f;\n        }\n    }\n\n    switch (failure) {\n        case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); Padded up to a multiple of TILE so the\n// vectorized kernel never needs a scalar remainder loop; padding entries are\n// never used as force sources and their results are discarded.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    size_t n = 0; i < bodies.n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    const size_t nPad = bodies.nPad;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < nPad; i0 += TILE) {\n        double xi[TILE], yi[TILE], zi[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            xi[t] = px[i0 + t];\n            yi[t] = py[i0 + t];\n            zi[t] = pz[i0 + t];\n            Fx[t] = 0.0;\n            Fy[t] = 0.0;\n            Fz[t] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j];\n            const double yj = py[j];\n            const double zj = pz[j];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[t];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[t];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[t];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[t] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[t] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[t] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t tEnd = (i0 + TILE <= n) ? i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n#pragma omp parallel for simd reduction(+ : energy) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (vx[i] * vx[i] + vy[i] * vy[i] + vz[i] * vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies).\n    // The i-loop is triangular, so a dynamic schedule balances the work.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : energy) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double local = 0.0;\n#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : local)\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - px[i];\n            const double dy = py[j] - py[i];\n            const double dz = pz[j] - pz[i];\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            local -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += local;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    // 1: non-finite, 2: position bounds, 3: velocity bounds, 0: ok\n    const auto check = [&](const size_t i) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px[i]) || !std::isfinite(py[i]) || !std::isfinite(pz[i]) || !std::isfinite(vx[i]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy[i]) || !std::isfinite(vz[i])) {\n            return 1;\n        }\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(px[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(py[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(pz[i]) > maxPos) {\n            return 2;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(vx[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(vy[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(vz[i]) > maxVel) {\n            return 3;\n        }\n        return 0;\n    };\n\n    // Report the first (lowest-index) failure, as the sequential version did.\n    long long firstBad = (long long)n;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : firstBad) schedule(static)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < (long long)n; ++i) {\n        if (check((size_t)i) != 0 && i < firstBad) {\n            firstBad = i;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const int failure = firstBad < (long long)n ? check((size_t)firstBad) : 0;\n    switch (failure) {\n        case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n        case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        case 3: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        default: return true;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Initialize bodies\n    BodySystem bodies(numBodies > 0 ? Padded up to a multiple of TILE so the\n// vectorized kernel never needs a scalar remainder loop; padding entries are\n// never used as force sources and their results are discarded.\n//\n// Positions are double buffered: a step reads the positions of buffer `cur`\n// and writes the integrated positions to the other one. That removes the\n// write-after-read dependency between the force loop and the integration, so\n// a step needs a single barrier instead of two.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    size_t n = 0;    // number of real bodies\n    size_t nPad = 0; // n rounded up to a multiple of TILE\n    size_t cur = 0;  // position buffer holding the current positions\n    std::vector<double> px[2], py[2], pz[2];\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz;\n\n    explicit BodySystem(const size_t numBodies)\n        : n(numBodies), nPad(((numBodies + TILE - 1) / TILE) * TILE), px{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0),\n                                                                        std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)},\n          py{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0), std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)},\n          pz{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0), std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)}, vx(nPad, 0.0), vy(nPad, 0.0),\n          vz(nPad, 0.0) {}\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: reproduces the exact rand_r() stream of the original.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Force computation of one step, fused with the position integration of the\n// bodies owned by the calling thread. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region; the `omp for` distributes the tiles and the caller's barrier\n// separates the position writes from the next step's position reads.\nstatic void computeStep(const BodySystem& bodies, const size_t readBuf) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    const size_t nPad = bodies.nPad;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px[readBuf].data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py[readBuf].data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz[readBuf].data();\n    double* __restrict qx = const_cast<double*>(bodies.px[readBuf ^ 1].data());\n    double* __restrict qy = const_cast<double*>(bodies.py[readBuf ^ 1].data());\n    double* __restrict qz = const_cast<double*>(bodies.pz[readBuf ^ 1].data());\n    double* __restrict vx = const_cast<double*>(bodies.vx.data());\n    double* __restrict vy = const_cast<double*>(bodies.vy.data());\n    double* __restrict vz = const_cast<double*>(bodies.vz.data());\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < nPad; i0 += TILE) {\n        double xi[TILE], yi[TILE], zi[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            xi[t] = px[i0 + t];\n            yi[t] = py[i0 + t];\n            zi[t] = pz[i0 + t];\n            Fx[t] = 0.0;\n            Fy[t] = 0.0;\n            Fz[t] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; Padded up to a multiple of TILE so the\n// vectorized kernel never needs a scalar remainder loop; padding entries are\n// never used as force sources and their results are discarded.\n//\n// Positions are double buffered: a step reads the positions of buffer `cur`\n// and writes the integrated positions to the other one. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region; the `omp for` distributes the tiles and the caller's barrier\n// separates the position writes from the next step's position reads.\nstatic void computeStep(const BodySystem& bodies, const size_t readBuf) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    const size_t nPad = bodies.nPad;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px[readBuf].data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py[readBuf].data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz[readBuf].data();\n    double* __restrict qx = const_cast<double*>(bodies.px[readBuf ^ 1].data());\n    double* __restrict qy = const_cast<double*>(bodies.py[readBuf ^ 1].data());\n    double* __restrict qz = const_cast<double*>(bodies.pz[readBuf ^ 1].data());\n    double* __restrict vx = const_cast<double*>(bodies.vx.data());\n    double* __restrict vy = const_cast<double*>(bodies.vy.data());\n    double* __restrict vz = const_cast<double*>(bodies.vz.data());\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; TILE : n - i0;\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < tEnd; ++t) {\n            const size_t i = i0 + t;\n            // Velocity update (computeForces) followed by the integration of\n            // this body's position; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * std::fma(vz[i], vz[i], std::fma(vx[i], vx[i], vy[i] * vy[i]));\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies).\n    // The i-loop is triangular, so a dynamic schedule balances the work.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : energy) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < (long long)n; ++i) {\n        if (check((size_t)i) != 0 && i < firstBad) {\n            firstBad = i;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const int failure = firstBad < (long long)n ? check((size_t)firstBad) : 0;\n    switch (failure) {\n        case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); The kernel is limited by the divide/square-root units,\n// which are shared by the SMT siblings of a core, so using more than one\n// thread per core only adds barrier cost.\nstatic int smtDegree() {\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) {\n        return 1;\n    }\n    char buf[512] = {};\n    const size_t len = fread(buf, 1, sizeof(buf) - 1, f);\n    fclose(f);\n\n    // The list is comma separated and may contain ranges, e.g. \"0,128\" or \"0-1\"\n    int count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < len;) {\n        char* endA = nullptr;\n        const long a = strtol(buf + i, &endA, 10);\n        if (endA == buf + i) {\n            break;\n        }\n        i = (size_t)(endA - buf);\n        long b = a;\n        if (i < len && buf[i] == '-') {\n            char* endB = nullptr;\n            b = strtol(buf + i + 1, &endB, 10);\n            i = (size_t)(endB - buf);\n        }\n        count += (int)(b - a + 1);\n        while (i < len && (buf[i] == ',' || buf[i] == ' ' || buf[i] == '\\n')) {\n            ++i;\n        }\n    }\n    return count > 0 ? Padded up to a multiple of TILE so the\n// vectorized kernel never needs a scalar remainder loop; padding entries are\n// never used as force sources and their results are discarded.\n//\n// Positions are double buffered: a step reads the positions of buffer `cur`\n// and writes the integrated positions to the other one. That removes the\n// write-after-read dependency between the force loop and the integration, so\n// a step needs a single barrier instead of two.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    size_t n = 0;    // number of real bodies\n    size_t nPad = 0; // n rounded up to a multiple of TILE\n    size_t cur = 0;  // position buffer holding the current positions\n    std::vector<double> px[2], py[2], pz[2];\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz;\n\n    explicit BodySystem(const size_t numBodies)\n        : n(numBodies), nPad(((numBodies + TILE - 1) / TILE) * TILE), px{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0),\n                                                                        std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)},\n          py{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0), std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)},\n          pz{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0), std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)}, vx(nPad, 0.0), vy(nPad, 0.0),\n          vz(nPad, 0.0) {}\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: reproduces the exact rand_r() stream of the original.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Force computation of one step, fused with the position integration of the\n// bodies owned by the calling thread. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region; the `omp for` distributes the tiles and the caller's barrier\n// separates the position writes from the next step's position reads.\nstatic void computeStep(BodySystem& bodies, const size_t readBuf) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    const size_t nPad = bodies.nPad;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px[readBuf].data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py[readBuf].data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz[readBuf].data();\n    double* __restrict qx = bodies.px[readBuf ^ 1].data();\n    double* __restrict qy = bodies.py[readBuf ^ 1].data();\n    double* __restrict qz = bodies.pz[readBuf ^ 1].data();\n    double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < nPad; i0 += TILE) {\n        double xi[TILE], yi[TILE], zi[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n            xi[t] = px[i0 + t];\n            yi[t] = py[i0 + t];\n            zi[t] = pz[i0 + t];\n            Fx[t] = 0.0;\n            Fy[t] = 0.0;\n            Fz[t] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j];\n            const double yj = py[j];\n            const double zj = pz[j];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[t];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[t];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[t];\n                const double distSqr = distSqrSoft(dx, dy, dz);\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[t] = std::fma(dx, invDist3, Fx[t]);\n                Fy[t] = std::fma(dy, invDist3, Fy[t]);\n                Fz[t] = std::fma(dz, invDist3, Fz[t]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t tEnd = (i0 + TILE <= n) ? TILE : n - i0;\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < tEnd; ++t) {\n            const size_t i = i0 + t;\n            // Velocity update (computeForces) followed by the integration of\n            // this body's position; both only touch body i.\n            vx[i] += DT * Fx[t];\n            vy[i] += DT * Fy[t];\n            vz[i] += DT * Fz[t];\n            qx[i] = std::fma(vx[i], DT, xi[t]);\n            qy[i] = std::fma(vy[i], DT, yi[t]);\n            qz[i] = std::fma(vz[i], DT, zi[t]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid runSimulation(BodySystem& bodies, const int numSteps) {\n    const size_t startBuf = bodies.cur;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n            computeStep(bodies, (startBuf + (size_t)step) & 1);\n#pragma omp barrier\n        }\n    }\n\n    bodies.cur = (startBuf + (size_t)numSteps) & 1;\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px[bodies.cur].data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py[bodies.cur].data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz[bodies.cur].data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n#pragma omp parallel for simd reduction(+ : energy) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * std::fma(vz[i], vz[i], std::fma(vx[i], vx[i], vy[i] * vy[i]));\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies).\n    // The i-loop is triangular, so a dynamic schedule balances the work.\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(+ : energy) schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double local = 0.0;\n#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : local)\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - px[i];\n            const double dy = py[j] - py[i];\n            const double dz = pz[j] - pz[i];\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(distSqrSoft(dx, dy, dz));\n            local -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += local;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px[bodies.cur].data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py[bodies.cur].data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz[bodies.cur].data();\n    const double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n    // 1: non-finite, 2: position bounds, 3: velocity bounds, 0: ok\n    const auto check = [&](const size_t i) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(px[i]) || !std::isfinite(py[i]) || !std::isfinite(pz[i]) || !std::isfinite(vx[i]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(vy[i]) || !std::isfinite(vz[i])) {\n            return 1;\n        }\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(px[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(py[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(pz[i]) > maxPos) {\n            return 2;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(vx[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(vy[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(vz[i]) > maxVel) {\n            return 3;\n        }\n        return 0;\n    };\n\n    // Report the first (lowest-index) failure, as the sequential version did.\n    long long firstBad = (long long)n;\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : firstBad) schedule(static)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < (long long)n; ++i) {\n        if (check((size_t)i) != 0 && i < firstBad) {\n            firstBad = i;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const int failure = firstBad < (long long)n ? check((size_t)firstBad) : 0;\n    switch (failure) {\n        case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n        case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        case 3: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); Padded up to a multiple of TILE so the\n// vectorized kernel never needs a scalar remainder loop; padding entries are\n// never used as force sources and their results are discarded.\n//\n// Positions are double buffered: a step reads the positions of buffer `cur`\n// and writes the integrated positions to the other one. That removes the\n// write-after-read dependency between the force loop and the integration, so\n// a step needs a single barrier instead of two.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    size_t n = 0;    // number of real bodies\n    size_t nPad = 0; // n rounded up to a multiple of TILE\n    size_t cur = 0;  // position buffer holding the current positions\n    std::vector<double> px[2], py[2], pz[2];\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz;\n\n    explicit BodySystem(const size_t numBodies)\n        : n(numBodies), nPad(((numBodies + TILE - 1) / TILE) * TILE), px{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0),\n                                                                        std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)},\n          py{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0), std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)},\n          pz{std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0), std::vector<double>(nPad, 0.0)}, vx(nPad, 0.0), vy(nPad, 0.0),\n          vz(nPad, 0.0) {}\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: reproduces the exact rand_r() stream of the original.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[0][i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Force computation of one step, fused with the position integration of the\n// bodies owned by the calling thread. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region; the `omp for` distributes the tiles and the caller's barrier\n// separates the position writes from the next step's position reads.\nstatic void computeStep(BodySystem& bodies, const size_t readBuf) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.n;\n    const size_t nPad = bodies.nPad;\n\n    const double* __restrict px = bodies.px[readBuf].data();\n    const double* __restrict py = bodies.py[readBuf].data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = bodies.pz[readBuf].data();\n    double* __restrict qx = bodies.px[readBuf ^ 1].data();\n    double* __restrict qy = bodies.py[readBuf ^ 1].data();\n    double* __restrict qz = bodies.pz[readBuf ^ 1].data();\n    double* __restrict vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static) nowait\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j];\n            const double yj = py[j];\n            const double zj = pz[j];\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < TILE; ++t) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[t];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[t];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[t];\n                const double distSqr = distSqrSoft(dx, dy, dz);\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[t] = std::fma(dx, invDist3, Fx[t]);\n                Fy[t] = std::fma(dy, invDist3, Fy[t]);\n                Fz[t] = std::fma(dz, invDist3, Fz[t]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t tEnd = (i0 + TILE <= n) ? TILE : n - i0;\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < tEnd; ++t) {\n            const size_t i = i0 + t;\n            // Velocity update (computeForces) followed by the integration of\n            // this body's position; both only touch body i.\n            vx[i] += DT * Fx[t];\n            vy[i] += DT * Fy[t];\n            vz[i] += DT * Fz[t];\n            qx[i] = std::fma(vx[i], DT, xi[t]);\n            qy[i] = std::fma(vy[i], DT, yi[t]);\n            qz[i] = std::fma(vz[i], DT, zi[t]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid runSimulation(BodySystem& bodies, const int numSteps) {\n    const size_t startBuf = bodies.cur;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int step = 0; i < (long long)n; ++i) {\n        if (check((size_t)i) != 0 && i < firstBad) {\n            firstBad = i;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const int failure = firstBad < (long long)n ? check((size_t)firstBad) : 0;\n    switch (failure) {\n        case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n        case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        case 3: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        default: return true;\n    }\n}\n\n// Number of hardware threads sharing one physical core (SMT degree), 1 if it\n// cannot be determined. The kernel is limited by the divide/square-root units,\n// which are shared by the SMT siblings of a core, so using more than one\n// thread per core only adds barrier cost.\nstatic int smtDegree() {\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) {\n        return 1;\n    }\n    char buf[512] = {};\n    const size_t len = fread(buf, 1, sizeof(buf) - 1, f);\n    fclose(f);\n\n    // The list is comma separated and may contain ranges, e.g. \"0,128\" or \"0-1\"\n    int count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; Each lane accumulates one body's force over all *j* in the original sequential order, so vectorization introduces no reassociation. Tiles are distributed with `#pragma omp for schedule(static)`.\n\n**One barrier per step** — the whole step loop runs in a single `#pragma omp parallel` region (no fork/join per step), and each thread integrates the positions of the bodies it just computed forces for, writing into the other position buffer (`for ... nowait` + one explicit `barrier`). Buffer parity is derived from the step index, so no swap/`single` is needed.\n\n**Thread count (`chooseThreadCount`)** — capped by `OMP_NUM_THREADS`, by one thread per *physical* core (SMT siblings share the divide/sqrt units, so they add only barrier cost), and by a minimum work-per-thread so small *n* doesn't drown in barriers. Unbounded 256 threads was 2–7x *slower* than 128 on this 2×64-core EPYC.\n\nEnergy, validation, and result serialization are parallelized too (the potential-energy loop uses `schedule(dynamic)` for the triangular iteration space).\n\n## Correctness\n\nResults are **bit-identical to the original binary**, not just within tolerance. Getting there required pinning FP contraction: the kernels now spell out fused multiply-adds with `std::fma()` in exactly the pattern GCC chose for the scalar original, and the target is compiled with `-ffp-contract=off` so rounding no longer depends on how the loop is vectorized. `-fno-math-errno` was needed for `sqrt` to vectorize at all (it was silently blocking vectorization, worth 4x).\n\nVerified: hashes match `/tmp/ref/orig` (a rebuild of the original) for n ∈ {1,2,7,8,9,100,1024,3000,4096,5000} × steps ∈ {0,1,10}, and for 1–256 threads at n=3000.
